## 2026-08-11 — Amazon native download completion status

- Time: 00:03:36 (Asia/Karachi).
- Purpose: retain reliable browser-native Amazon file downloading while keeping Process visibly active until the server has finished sending the workbook response.
- Backups created: `app/main.py.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`; `templates/index.html.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`; `README.md.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`; `HUMAN_GUIDE.md.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`; `output/wallmartsummary_workflow_documentation.html.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`; `edit log/ai edit history.txt.2026-08-11.amazon-native-download-status.bak`.
- File: `app/main.py`. Amazon Step 01 assigns a browser-supplied download token, redirects the native form request to its generated file, and marks the token complete only after the FileResponse has finished sending. Added a read-only Amazon download-status route for the page to check this completion state.
- File: `templates/index.html`. Keeps the Amazon form as a native submission, generates a token before submit, and polls the completion route every 250ms. The button remains `Processing...` until the server reports the native download response complete; no response/blob download code or fixed reset timeout is used.
- File: `README.md`; `HUMAN_GUIDE.md`; `output/wallmartsummary_workflow_documentation.html`. Documented the server-complete native download state.
- Research: MDN confirms that `Response.blob()` reads a Fetch response body to completion, while browser download behavior differs by browser/settings. FastAPI FileResponse supports streamed file responses and Content-Disposition filenames. The implementation avoids the browser-specific blob-download behavior that failed in this environment.
- Verification: `python -m py_compile app/main.py` passed. Rendered Amazon template JavaScript syntax check passed.
- Route/button impact: Amazon `Process Amazon File` now retains native download and a server-completion busy state. WallMartSummary routes, SQL, values, and procedures remain unchanged.

- File: `app/adapters/wallmart_python_processor.py`. Blank Comment leaves all comment-based helpers blank while InvYear continues from Date. Unpaid and InvPaid apply only to IN rows. ## 2026-10-04 — Regular Accounts: skip tiny pivots, case-sensitive person match, local.db parse cache

Purpose:

Files changed:
- `app/excel_pivot_builder.py`: added pivot_skipped_types(); the Excel script skips the IN/CR/DB (and DB-by-reason) PivotTable when that Ref Type has fewer than 2 rows.
- `app/main.py` (_build_regular_accounts_report): adds a NOTICE sheet listing skipped pivots and logs a warning; imports pivot_skipped_types.
- `app/adapters/regular_accounts_processor.py`: person match is exact-case (selected_person_rows); _full_ageing_rows now reads through cached_frame().
- `app/local_cache.py` (new): SQLite cache at app/local.db (table frame_cache, keyed by file-content SHA1, WAL mode, keeps newest 8 entries / 24 hours). Measured: repeat read 5.66 s -> 0.07 s, identical data.
- `edit log/ai edit history.txt`: appended this record only.

- /process/regular-accounts/people and /process/regular-accounts/follow-up-report only. No buttons or other routes changed.

- Not changed: Excel pivot build time for people with many accounts (still subject to the 120 s limit in excel_pivot_builder.py).

## 2026-10-04 — Progress panel, faster Regular Accounts pivots, Amazon download route restored

Purpose:
- Show users how much of a process is done and which file/step is running. Fix the slow Excel setup step. Restore a route removed by mistake.

Files changed:
- `app/progress.py` (new): in-memory job progress (percent, stage, done, error) keyed by a token.
- `app/DbConfigLogger.py`: Logger gained progress_token; each log message advances the bar and becomes the current step text.
- `app/main.py`: new GET /progress/status; progress updates in the Regular Accounts report (new download_token form field and token passed to _build_regular_accounts_report), Wal Mart and Amazon (_run_step_01_flow), and New Ageing. The Regular Accounts, New Ageing and Step 01 routes are now plain `def` routes so they run in worker threads and /progress/status can answer during processing. Restored GET /download/amazon-account-sheets, _amazon_grouped_output_path, _mark_amazon_download_complete and the AMAZON_GROUPED_OUTPUT_FILE import: the Amazon flow redirects to that route when a download_token is sent, so removing it earlier broke Amazon downloads.
- `app/excel_pivot_builder.py`: New-RegularAccountsPivotSource now reads the sheet once, filters in memory and writes once (was several Excel calls per row). Measured on a 6,684-row person: 163-202 s (timed out at 120 s in the app) -> about 51 s.
